D.19
Proof of Proposition
5

From the proof of Lemma
12
, we have

\displaystyle V^{*}=V_{1}=\Theta_{n2}+A_{11}+A_{2},

and the terms on the right-hand-side are mutually uncorrelated.
From (
144
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(A_{11})\lesssim\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{N_{i}}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(A_{2})\lesssim\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}{N_{i}^{2}}.

Hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}V^{*}

\displaystyle=\Theta_{n2}

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{N_{i}}\vee\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}{N_{i}^{2}}.

(159)

Since
K=n
and the null hypothesis holds, we have
\Theta_{n1}\equiv\Theta_{n4}\equiv 0
. Moreover, by (
122
), we have

\displaystyle\Theta_{n3}\lesssim\|\mu\|^{2}\ll\Theta_{n2}\asymp n\|\mu\|^{2}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T)=[1+o(1)]\Theta_{n2}\asymp n\|\mu\|^{2}.

(160)

Thus by (
159
) and Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{V^{*}}{\mathrm{Var}(T)}=\frac{V^{*}-\mathbb{E}V^{*}}{\mathrm{Var}(T)}+\frac{\mathbb{E}V^{*}}{\mathrm{Var}(T)}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)+1+o(1),

as desired.

\qed

D.20
Proof of Proposition
6

By Lemmas
8
and
14
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T)\gtrsim\Theta_{n2}+\Theta_{n3}\gtrsim\sum_{i}\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}.

(161)

By (
159
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}{N_{i}^{2}}\vee\sum_{i}\frac{\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{N_{i}}

(162)

Using a similar argument based on Chebyshev’s inequality as in the proof of Proposition
1
and applying (
161
) and (
162
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{|V^{*}-\mathbb{E}V^{*}|}{\mathrm{Var}(T)}\gtrsim\frac{|V^{*}-\mathbb{E}V^{*}|}{\sum_{i}\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(163)

Next, by Lemma
12
and (
161
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}V^{*}=\Theta_{n2}\lesssim\mathrm{Var}(T).

(164)

Combining (
157
) and (
164
) finishes the proof.

\qed

Appendix E
Proofs of asymptotic normality results

The goal of this section is to prove Theorems
1
and
2
. The argument relies on the martingale central limit theorem and the lemmas stated below. As a preliminary, we describe a martingale decomposition of
T
under the null.

Define

\displaystyle U=\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}(U_{3}+U_{4}),\hskip 17.00024pt\text{and }\,\,\,S=\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}U_{2}.

By Lemma
3
, we have
T=U+S
under the null hypothesis. It holds that

\displaystyle U=\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\sigma_{i,i^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{s=1}^{N_{i^{\prime}}}\big(\sum_{j}Z_{ijr}Z_{i^{\prime}js}\big).

(165)

where we define

\sigma_{i,i^{\prime}}=\begin{cases}2\big(\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}-\frac{1}{n\bar{N}}\big)&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}\text{ for some }k\\
-\frac{2}{n\bar{N}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ else}.\end{cases}

Define a sequence of random variables

\displaystyle D_{\ell,s}=\sum_{i\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i,\ell}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{j}Z_{ijr}Z_{\ell js}

(166)

indexed by
(\ell,s)\in\{(i,r)\}_{1\leq i\leq n,1\leq r\leq N_{i}}
, where these tuples are placed in lexicographical order. Precisely, we define

(\ell_{1},s_{1})\prec(\ell_{2},s_{2})

if either

•

\ell_{1}<\ell_{2}
, or

•

\ell_{1}=\ell_{2}
and
s_{1}<s_{2}
.

Observe that

\sum_{\ell,s}D_{\ell,s}=U.

Next define
\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}
to be the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{Z_{i:r}\}_{(i,r)\prec(\ell,s)}
. Observe that

\mathbb{E}[D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}]=0,

and hence
\{D_{\ell,s}\}
is a martingale difference sequence.
Turning to
S
, we have

\displaystyle S=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}\sum_{r<s}\sum_{j}Z_{ijr}Z_{ijs}.

(167)

where we define

\sigma_{i}=2\Bigl(\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}-\frac{1}{n\bar{N}}\Bigr)\frac{N_{i}}{N_{i}-1}

if
i\in S_{k}
.
Define

\displaystyle E_{\ell,s}=\sigma_{\ell}\sum_{r\in[s-1]}\sum_{j}Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell js}.

(168)

Note that
E_{\ell,1}=0
. Order
(\ell,s)
lexicographically as above, and recall that
\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}
is the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{Z_{i:r}\}_{(i,r)\prec(\ell,s)}
. Observe that

\mathbb{E}[E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}]=0,

and hence
\{E_{\ell,s}\}
is a martingale difference sequence.
We have

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\sigma_{\ell}\sum_{r\in[s-1]}\sum_{j}Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell js}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\sum_{s=1}^{N_{\ell}}\sigma_{\ell}\sum_{r\in[s-1]}\sum_{j}Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell js}=S.

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}=D_{\ell,s}+E_{\ell,s},\hskip 8.50012pt\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}=\frac{\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(T)}}.

(169)

Thus we obtain the martingale decomposition:

\displaystyle T=U+S=\sum_{(\ell,s)}[D_{\ell,s}+E_{\ell,s}]=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}.

(170)

The technical results below are crucial to the proof of Theorem
1
given in Section
E.1
. Theorem
2
then follows easily from Theorem
1
and Theorem
1
.

Lemma 15

Let
\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}
be defined as in (
169
). It holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg]=1.

Lemma 16

Suppose that
\min N_{i}\geq 2
and
\max\|\Omega_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-c_{0}
. Under the null hypothesis, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})\bigg)\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}+K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

Lemma 17

Suppose that
\min N_{i}\geq 2
and
\max\|\Omega_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-c_{0}
. Under the null hypothesis, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}D_{\ell,s}^{4}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}^{2}\bar{N}_{k}^{2}}\big)\|\mu\|^{2}+\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}\,,

Lemma 18

Suppose that
\min N_{i}\geq 2
and and
\max\|\Omega_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-c_{0}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{E}_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})\bigg)\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{3}\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}\vee\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{4}\|\Omega_{i}\|_{4}^{4}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}

(171)

Lemma 19

Suppose that
\min N_{i}\geq 2
and and
\max\|\Omega_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-c_{0}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}\,E_{\ell,s}^{4}\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{2}\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}\vee\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{3}\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}

Lemma 20

Under either the null or alternative, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{2}\|\Omega_{i}\|^{2}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}^{2}\bar{N}_{k}^{2}}\|\mu_{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{3}\|\Omega_{i}\|_{3}^{3}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}\|\mu_{k}\|_{3}^{3}

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\frac{N_{i}^{4}\|\Omega_{i}\|_{4}^{4}}{n_{k}^{4}\bar{N}_{k}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\|\mu_{k}\|_{4}^{4}

E.1
Proof of Theorem
1

By the martingale central limit theorem (see e.g.
Hall and Heyde 2014
), we have that
T/\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(T)}\Rightarrow N(0,1)
if the following conditions are satisfied:

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\to}}1

(172)

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}^{2}{\bf 1}_{|\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}|>\varepsilon}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\to}}0,\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ for any }\varepsilon>0.

(173)

It is known that (
173
), which is a Lindeberg-type condition, is implied by the Lyapunov-type condition

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}^{4}=o(1).

(174)

See e.g.
jin2018network
.

Since (
23
) holds,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T)\gtrsim\Theta=\Theta_{n2}+\Theta_{n3}+\Theta_{n4}\gtrsim K\|\mu\|^{2}.

(175)

Recall that

\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}=\frac{\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}}{\mathrm{Var}(T)}=\frac{D_{\ell,s}+E_{\ell,s}}{\mathrm{Var}(T)},

Note that (
172
) holds if

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathrm{Var}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\to 1,\,\,\text{ and}

(176)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\mathrm{Var}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\to 0.

(177)

Recall that (
176
) holds by Lemma
15
.

Next note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(D_{\ell,s}E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})=0,

by inspection of the expressions for
D_{\ell,s}
and
E_{\ell,s}
in (
166
) and (
168
).
Therefore

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})=\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})+\mathrm{Var}(E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}).

Hence by (
175
); Lemmas
16
,
18
, and
20
; and the assumption (
26
), under the null hypothesis, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\mathrm{Var}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{M}}_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathrm{Var}(T)^{2}}\bigg[\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\mathrm{Var}\big(D_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg)+\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\mathrm{Var}\big(E_{\ell,s}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big)\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{K^{2}\|\mu\|^{4}}\bigg[\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}+K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}\big)\|\mu\|^{2}\bigg]=o(1).

This proves (
177
). Thus, (
176
) and (
177
) are established, which proves (
172
).

Similarly, (
174
) (and thus (
173
)) holds by (
175
); Lemmas (
17
), (
19
), and (
20
), and the assumption (
26
). Combining (
172
) and (
173
) verifies the conditions of the martingale central limit theorem, so we conclude that
T/\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(T)}\Rightarrow N(0,1)
. Since
\mathrm{Var}(T)=[1+o(1)]\Theta_{n}
by (
26
) and Lemma
9
, the proof is complete.

\qed

We record a useful proposition that records the weaker conditions under which
T/\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(T)}
is asymptotically normal.

Proposition 7

Recall that
\alpha_{n}
is defined as

\alpha_{n}:=\max\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\|\mu_{k}\|_{3}^{3}}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}},\hskip 8.50012pt\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\|\mu_{k}\|^{2}}{n_{k}^{2}\bar{N}_{k}^{2}}\right\}\bigg/\bigg(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\mu_{k}\|^{2}\bigg)^{2}

(178)

in (
24
). If under the null hypothesis,

\displaystyle\alpha_{n}=\max\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\|\mu_{k}\|_{3}^{3}}{n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}},\hskip 8.50012pt\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\|\mu_{k}\|^{2}}{n_{k}^{2}\bar{N}_{k}^{2}}\right\}\bigg/\bigg(K\|\mu\|^{2}\bigg)^{2}\to 0,\hskip 17.00024pt\text{ and }\,\,\frac{\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}}{K\|\mu\|^{4}}\to 0,

(179)

then
T/\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(T)}\Rightarrow N(0,1)
.

E.2
Proof of Theorem
2

By our assumptions, Proposition
1
holds, and
V/\mathrm{Var}(T)\to 1
. Thus the variance estimate
V
is consistent under the null. Theorem
2
then follows from Slutsky’s theorem and Theorem
1
.
\qed

E.3
Proof of Lemma
15

By Lemma
3
,
S
and
U
are uncorrelated, and it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(T)=\mathrm{Var}(S)+\mathrm{Var}(U).

(180)

Next note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(D_{\ell,s}E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})=0,

by inspection of the expressions for
D_{\ell,s}
and
E_{\ell,s}
in (
166
) and (
168
).
Therefore

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\mathcal{M}_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})=\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})+\mathrm{Var}(E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}).

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(E_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}E_{\ell,s}^{2}=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\sigma_{\ell}^{2}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}\in[s-1]}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell js}Z_{\ell j^{\prime}r^{\prime}}Z_{\ell j^{\prime}s}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\sigma_{\ell}^{2}\sum_{r\in[s-1]}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell j^{\prime}r}Z_{\ell js}Z_{\ell j^{\prime}s}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\sigma_{\ell}^{2}\sum_{s\in[N_{\ell}]}\sum_{r\in[s-1]}\mathbb{E}\big(\sum_{j}Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell js}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}(S).

(181)

The last line is obtained noting that
S
as defined in (
167
) is a sum of uncorrelated terms over
(i,r,s)
.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}\big[D_{\ell,s}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}\big]\bigg]=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathbb{E}\big[D_{\ell,s}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{(\ell,s)}\sum_{i\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i,\ell}^{2}\mathrm{Var}\big(\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{j}Z_{ijr}Z_{\ell js}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i,\ell}^{2}\mathrm{Var}\big(\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{s=1}^{N_{\ell}}Z_{ijr}Z_{\ell js}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}(U).

(182)

The lemma follows by combining (
180
)–(
182
).

\qed

E.4
Proof of Lemma
16

Let
M_{k}=n_{k}\bar{N}_{k}
and
M=n\bar{N}
. Define

\displaystyle\Sigma=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{k}M_{k}\Sigma_{k}=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}.

(183)

Our main goal is to control the conditional variance process. Define

\displaystyle\delta_{jj^{\prime}\ell}=\mathbb{E}Z_{\ell jr}Z_{\ell j^{\prime}r}=\begin{cases}\Omega_{\ell j}(1-\Omega_{\ell j})&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j=j^{\prime}\\
-\Omega_{\ell j}\Omega_{\ell j^{\prime}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ else}.\end{cases}

(184)

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in[\ell-1]}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in[\ell-1]}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in[\ell-1]}\sum_{r,r^{\prime}}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}

Define

\displaystyle\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}=\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}.

(185)

Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell,s}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in[\ell-1]}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i^{\prime}}}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}\,Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i}}\,\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\bigg(\sum_{\ell>i}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}^{2}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}\,\bigg)Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i^{\prime}}}\,\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\bigg(\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}\,\bigg)Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i}}\,\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i^{\prime}}}\,\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}.

Define

\displaystyle\zeta_{iri^{\prime}r^{\prime}}=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}.

(186)

Then

\displaystyle\sum_{(\ell,s)}\mathrm{Var}(D_{\ell,s}|\mathcal{F}_{\prec(\ell,s)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\zeta_{irir}+\bigg(2\sum_{i}\sum_{r<r^{\prime}\in[N_{i}]}\zeta_{irir^{\prime}}+2\sum_{i<i^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{N_{i^{\prime}}}\zeta_{iri^{\prime}r^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=:V_{1}+V_{2}

With this decomposition, Lemma
16
follows directly from Lemmas
21
and
22
stated below and proved in the next remainder of this subsection.

Lemma 21

It holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{1})\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

Lemma 22

It holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

\qed

E.4.1
Statement and proof of Lemma
23

The proofs of Lemmas
21
and
22
heavily rely on the following intermediate result that bounds the coefficients
\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}
in all cases.

Lemma 23

It holds that

\displaystyle\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{cases}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k},j_{1}=j_{2}\\
\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k},j_{1}\neq j_{2}\\
\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }i\in S_{k_{1}},i^{\prime}\in S_{k_{2}},k_{1}\neq k_{2},j_{1}=j_{2}\\
\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a=1}^{2}\Sigma_{k_{a}j_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }i\in S_{k_{1}},i^{\prime}\in S_{k_{2}},k_{1}\neq k_{2},j_{1}\neq j_{2}\end{cases}

Proof E.1
.

If
j_{1}=j_{2}
and
i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{1}\ell}|\leq\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\sum_{\ell\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{1}\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{k}}\cdot\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{\ell\in S_{k}}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}+\frac{1}{M}\cdot\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}+\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}.

If
j_{1}\neq j_{2}
and
i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}|\leq\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}|\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}|\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{k}}\cdot\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{\ell\in S_{k}}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\cdot\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}\lesssim\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}.

If
i\neq i^{\prime}
,
j_{1}=j_{2}
, and
i\in S_{k_{1}},i^{\prime}\in S_{k_{2}}
where
k_{1}\neq k_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{1}\ell}|\leq\sum_{\ell}N_{\ell}|\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}|\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{M}\cdot\sum_{a=1}^{2}\frac{1}{M_{k_{a}}}\sum_{\ell\in S_{k_{a}}}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}+\frac{1}{M}\cdot\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}=\frac{3}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}.

If
i\neq i^{\prime}
,
j_{1}\neq j_{2}
, and
i\in S_{k_{1}},i^{\prime}\in S_{k_{2}}
where
k_{1}\neq k_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{\ell>i^{\prime}}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\delta_{j_{1}j_{2}\ell}|\lesssim\sum_{\ell}N_{\ell}\sigma_{i\ell}\sigma_{i^{\prime}\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{M}\cdot\sum_{a=1}^{2}\frac{1}{M_{k_{a}}}\sum_{\ell\in S_{k_{a}}}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\cdot\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\ell\in[n]}N_{\ell}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a=1}^{2}\Sigma_{k_{a}j_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}.

E.4.2
Proof of Lemma
21

We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{1})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,r}\mathbb{E}\zeta_{irir}^{2}.

Next by symmetry,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\zeta_{irir}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{4}}\,\mathbb{E}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{ij_{3}r}Z_{ij_{2}r}Z_{ij_{4}r}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{3}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}=:\sum_{a=1}^{7}B_{a,i,r}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{1})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{a}\bigg(\underbrace{\sum_{i,r}B_{a,i,r}}_{=:B_{a}}\bigg).

We analyze
B_{1}
–
B_{7}
separately, bounding the
\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{r}j_{s}}
coefficients using Lemma
23
.

For
B_{1}
,

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,r}\sum_{j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1}}(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}})^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}}(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}})^{2}M_{k}\mu_{j_{1}}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(187)

For
B_{2}
,

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,r}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\cdot\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\cdot M_{k}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}+\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{\mathbf{1}^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu}{M_{k}}+\sum_{k}\frac{\mathbf{1}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{k}\circ\Sigma)\mu}{M}=\sum_{k}\frac{\mathbf{1}^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu}{M_{k}}

Next,

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}\cdot\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\sum_{j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\cdot\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{1}}^{3}=\|\mu\|_{3}^{3},

(188)

and similarly

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}M}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in[n]}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}\cdot\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}}\frac{1}{M_{k}M}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in[n]}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\mu_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{1}}^{3}=\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

Thus

\displaystyle B_{2}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(189)

For
B_{3}
,

\displaystyle B_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,r}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{3}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{3}}\cdot\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{3}}\cdot M_{k}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}\mu}{M_{k}}.

We have by Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\mu^{\prime}\Omega_{i}\Omega_{i^{\prime}}^{\prime}\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}\Omega_{ij}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\big(\sum_{j}\Omega_{ij}\big)\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{2}\Omega_{ij}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{3}=\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(190)

Thus

\displaystyle B_{3}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}

(191)

For
B_{4}
,

\displaystyle B_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,r}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)^{2}\,\cdot M_{k}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{{\bf{1}}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 3}){\bf{1}}}{M_{k}}+\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}}{\bf{1}}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{k}\circ\Sigma^{\circ 2}){\bf{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{{\bf{1}}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 3}){\bf{1}}}{M_{k}}\big)+\frac{1}{M}\mathbf{1}^{\prime}(\Sigma^{\circ 3})\mathbf{1}.

First,

\displaystyle{\bf{1}}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 3}){\bf{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\big(\sum_{j}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\Omega_{i_{3}j}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\cdot\sum_{j}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\Omega_{i_{3}j}=\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{3}=\|\mu\|_{3}^{3},

and similarly,

\displaystyle{\bf{1}}^{\prime}(\Sigma^{\circ 3}){\bf{1}}=\frac{1}{M^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\in[n]}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\big(\sum_{j}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\Omega_{i_{3}j}\big)^{2}\leq\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

Thus

\displaystyle B_{4}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}

(192)

For
B_{5}
,

\displaystyle B_{5}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i,r}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{iij_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{iij_{3}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}})\cdot\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{N_{i}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}^{2}}+\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{N_{i}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}M}

\displaystyle=:B_{51}+B_{52}.

We have

\displaystyle B_{51}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}(\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mu)\cdot(\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\Omega_{i_{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\cdot\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mu\cdot\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\Omega_{i_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}}N_{i_{1}}\mu^{\prime}\Omega_{i_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mu=\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}\mu\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(193)

In the last line we apply (
190
). Similarly,

\displaystyle B_{52}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}M^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}\in S_{k},i_{2}\in[n]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}M^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}\in S_{k},i_{2}\in[n]}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\cdot\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mu\cdot\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\Omega_{i_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}M}\sum_{i_{1}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}\mu^{\prime}\Omega_{i_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}}^{\prime}\mu\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(194)

Thus

\displaystyle B_{5}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(195)

For
B_{6}
,

\displaystyle B_{6}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}(dist.)}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}^{2}}+2\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}M}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M^{2}}=:B_{61}+B_{62}+B_{63}.

First,

\displaystyle B_{61}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}}{M_{k}^{2}}=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mathbf{1}^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3},

where we applied (
188
). Similarly,

\displaystyle B_{62}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3},\text{ and}

\displaystyle B_{63}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

Thus

\displaystyle B_{6}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(196)

For
B_{7}
, we have

\displaystyle B_{7}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\big)\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}M}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M^{2}}=:B_{71}+B_{72}+B_{73}.

Note that

\displaystyle\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\Omega_{ij_{1}}=\mu_{j_{1}},\text{ and}

\displaystyle\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i\in[n]}N_{i}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i\in[n]}N_{i}\Omega_{ij_{1}}=\mu_{j_{1}}.

(197)

Thus

\displaystyle B_{71}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{r\in[N_{i}]}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{N_{i}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{ij_{4}}}{M_{k}^{2}}\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}

where we applied (
193
). Similarly,

\displaystyle B_{72}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3},\text{ and}

\displaystyle B_{73}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

Thus

\displaystyle B_{7}\lesssim\big(\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\big)\|\mu\|_{3}^{3}.

(198)

Combining the results for
B_{1}
–
B_{7}
concludes the proof.

∎

E.4.3
Proof of Lemma
22

We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{2})\lesssim 4\sum_{(i,r)\neq(i^{\prime},r^{\prime})}\mathbb{E}\zeta_{irir^{\prime}}^{2},

where
r\in[N_{i}]
and
r\in[N_{i^{\prime}}]
in the summation above.

By symmetry, if
(i,r)\neq(i^{\prime},r^{\prime})
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\zeta_{iri^{\prime}r^{\prime}}^{2}=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{4}}\,\mathbb{E}Z_{ij_{1}r}Z_{ij_{3}r}\,\mathbb{E}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{2}r^{\prime}}Z_{i^{\prime}j_{4}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{3}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{3}}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}=:\sum_{a}^{7}C_{a,i,r}.

(199)

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(V_{2})\lesssim\sum_{a=1}^{7}\sum_{(i,r)\neq(i^{\prime},r^{\prime})}C_{a,i,r}\lesssim\sum_{a=1}^{7}\underbrace{\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}C_{a,i,r}}_{=:C_{a}}.

Next we analyze
C_{1},\ldots,C_{7}
, bounding the
\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{r}j_{s}}
coefficients using Lemma
23
.

For
C_{1}
,

\displaystyle C_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}\sum_{j_{1}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}})^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}\sum_{j_{1}}(\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}})^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{1}}^{4}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}^{4}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(200)

For
C_{2}
,

\displaystyle C_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}+\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{4}}\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{2}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}+\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}}\big(\mu_{j_{1}}^{4}+\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}^{4}\big)+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}^{4}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(201)

where we applied (
197
).

For
C_{3}
,

\displaystyle C_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{3}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{3}}\cdot\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{3}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{3}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{3}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}^{2}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\sum_{k}\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu\big)+\mu^{\prime}\Sigma^{\circ 2}\mu.

First, by Cauchy–Schwarz,

\displaystyle\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\big(\sum_{j}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}\big)\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{2}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{2}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}=\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{4}=\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(202)

Similarly

\displaystyle\mu^{\prime}\Sigma^{\circ 2}\mu\lesssim\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(203)

Hence

\displaystyle C_{3}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(204)

For
C_{4}
,

\displaystyle C_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)^{2}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}=:C_{41}+C_{42}

First,

\displaystyle C_{41}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}+\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}+\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\frac{M_{k}^{2}}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}\leq\sum_{k}\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu+\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}^{2}}{M^{2}}\mu^{\prime}\Sigma^{\circ 2}\mu.

Similarly,

\displaystyle C_{42}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\big(\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu+\mu^{\prime}\Sigma^{\circ 2}\mu\big)

Combining the previous two displays and applying (
202
) and (
203
), we have

\displaystyle C_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(205)

For
C_{5}
,

\displaystyle C_{5}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}(dist.)}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{1}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\mu_{j_{1}}\cdot\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=C_{51}+C_{52}+2C_{53}+C_{54}

For
C_{51}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{51}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle\cdot\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{k}C_{511k}^{1/2}\cdot C_{512k}^{1/2}.

(206)

We have by Cauchy–Schwarz that

\displaystyle C_{511k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{2}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\big)\big(\sum_{j}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{2}j}\big)\leq\|\mu\|_{4}^{4},

and similarly

\displaystyle C_{512k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\big(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{2}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\big(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{2}}\big)\big(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\big(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}^{2}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{2}}\big)=\mu^{\prime}\,\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\,\mu

(207)

Since by Cauchy–Schwarz,

\displaystyle\mu^{\prime}\,\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\,\mu

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i\in S_{k}}N_{i}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\big)^{2}=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\mu_{j_{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}\big(\sum_{j}\mu_{j}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}\big)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}\sum_{j}\mu_{j}^{2}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j}\leq\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

(208)

we have in total

C_{512k}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}
. Combining the result with the bound for
C_{511k}
implies that

\displaystyle C_{51}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

Next we study
C_{52}
using a similar argument.

\displaystyle C_{52}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i_{1}\in[n]}N_{i_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{2}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{3}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i_{3}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}M_{k}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in[n]\\
i_{2},i_{3}\in S_{k}\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}\in[S_{k}]}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\bigg(\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}\in[S_{k}]}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}\in[S_{k}]}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{k}C_{521k}^{1/2}C_{522k}^{1/2}.

(209)

Observe that
C_{521k}=C_{511k}
, and thus
C_{521}\lesssim\|\mu\|^{4}
by (
207
). With a similar argument as in (
208
) we obtain
C_{522k}\lesssim\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}
. Hence we obtain

\displaystyle C_{52}\leq\sum_{k}C_{521k}^{1/2}C_{522k}^{1/2}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

For
C_{53}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{53}=\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\mu_{j_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i_{1}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\sum_{i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{2}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{3}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i_{3}\in[n]}N_{i_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}M_{k}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}\\
i_{3}\in[n]\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{2}\in S_{k},i_{3}\in[n]}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle.

(210)

We then upper bound the last line using a similar strategy as in that we used for
C_{51}
and
C_{52}
, respectively. We omit the details and state the final bound:

\displaystyle C_{53}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

(211)

Finally for
C_{54}
, summing over
k,k^{\prime}
we obtain

\displaystyle C_{54}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}=\frac{1}{M^{3}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}\in[n]}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}\langle\mu\circ\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle.

(212)

We then proceed as in (
210
) to control the right-hand side. We omit the details and state the final bound:

\displaystyle C_{54}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(213)

Combining the results for
C_{51},\ldots,C_{54}
, we see that

\displaystyle C_{5}\lesssim K\|\mu\|^{4}.

For
C_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{6}\leq\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{4}}\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k\neq k^{\prime}\\
i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}\\
j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}\end{subarray}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\big)\,\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=:C_{61}+C_{62}.

For
C_{61}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{61}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle+\hskip 8.50012pt\sum_{k}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}=:C_{611}+2C_{612}+C_{613}.

Relabeling indices, we see that

\displaystyle C_{611}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}=C_{51}

Hence,
C_{611}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}
. Next,

\displaystyle C_{612}\leq\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}\lesssim K\|\mu\|^{4},

where we applied (
210
). Similarly,

\displaystyle C_{613}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}^{2}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}\leq\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{4}}\mu_{j_{1}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{2}j_{4}}\lesssim K\|\mu\|^{4},

where in the final bound we apply (
212
) and (
213
). Combining the results above for
C_{611},C_{612},C_{613}
, we obtain

\displaystyle C_{61}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

(214)

The argument for
C_{62}
is very similar, so we omit proof and state the final bound. We have

\displaystyle C_{62}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}.

Thus

\displaystyle C_{6}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

For
C_{7}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{7}\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\alpha_{ii^{\prime}j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M_{k}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\big)\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\\
i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{1}j_{2}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\big)\big(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{a\in\{k,k^{\prime}\}}^{2}\Sigma_{aj_{3}j_{4}}+\frac{1}{M}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\big)\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=:C_{71}+C_{72}

Write

\displaystyle C_{71}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+2\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M_{k}M}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{k}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in S_{k}}N_{i}N_{i^{\prime}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Omega_{ij_{1}}\Omega_{ij_{3}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{2}}\Omega_{i^{\prime}j_{4}}=:C_{711}+2C_{712}+C_{713}.

For
C_{711}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{711}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{4}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{k}\sum_{i_{3},i_{4}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\big(\Omega_{i_{3}}^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{4}}\big)^{2}=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{3},i_{4}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\big(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\Omega_{i_{3}j}^{\prime}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}\Omega_{i_{4}j^{\prime}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i_{3},i_{4}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\Omega_{i_{3}j}^{\prime}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}^{2}\Omega_{i_{4}j^{\prime}}\leq\sum_{k}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\mu_{j}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}^{2}\mu_{j^{\prime}}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(215)

In the last line we applied Cauchy–Schwarz and (
208
).
For
C_{712}
, we have similarly

\displaystyle C_{712}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}M_{k}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in[n]\\
i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k}\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}\in[n],i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{2}}\rangle^{2}\leq\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}\in[n],i_{2}\in S_{k}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}^{2}\Omega_{i_{2}j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}^{2}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\mu_{j}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}^{2}\mu_{j^{\prime}}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

(216)

Next,

\displaystyle C_{713}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}^{2}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{kj_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{4}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in[n]\\
i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k}\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle,

and applying a similar strategy as in (
215
), (
216
) leads to the bound
C_{713}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}
. Thus

\displaystyle C_{71}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

Next , by symmetry and summing over
i\in S_{k},i^{\prime}\in S_{k^{\prime}}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{72}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\bigg[2\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+2\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}+4\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}+\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\bigg]\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=:2C_{721}+2C_{722}+4C_{723}+C_{724}

First,

\displaystyle C_{721}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{j_{2}j_{4}}=C_{712}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

by (
216
). Next,

\displaystyle C_{722}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{kj_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{1}{M^{2}M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in S_{k}\\
i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}M_{k^{\prime}}}\sum_{i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle^{2}\leq\sum_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}}{M^{2}M_{k^{\prime}}}\sum_{i_{3},i_{4}\in S_{k^{\prime}}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}\Omega_{i_{3}j}\Sigma_{kjj^{\prime}}^{2}\Omega_{i_{4}j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\mu^{\prime}\Sigma_{k}^{\circ 2}\mu\leq\|\mu\|_{4}^{4},

(217)

where we applied Cauchy-Schwarz in the penultimate line and (
208
) in the last line.

For
C_{723}
, we have

\displaystyle C_{723}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{2}j_{4}}\leq\sum_{k}\frac{M_{k}}{M}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{j_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{3}M_{k}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{3}\in S_{k}\\
i_{2},i_{4}\in[n]\end{subarray}}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{3}\in S_{k},i_{4}\in[n]}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k}\frac{1}{M^{2}}\sum_{i_{3}\in S_{k},i_{4}\in[n]}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\big(\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma_{k}\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle^{2}+\langle\Omega_{i_{3}},\Sigma\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle^{2}\big)

Using a similar technique as in (
215
)–(
217
) and applying (
202
), (
203
) we obtain

\displaystyle C_{723}\lesssim\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

Finally, for
C_{724}
we have

\displaystyle C_{724}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq k^{\prime}}\frac{M_{k}M_{k^{\prime}}}{M^{2}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{kj_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{k^{\prime}j_{2}j_{4}}\leq\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{2}}\Sigma_{j_{3}j_{4}}\Sigma_{j_{1}j_{3}}\Sigma_{j_{2}j_{4}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M^{4}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in[n]}N_{i_{1}}N_{i_{2}}N_{i_{3}}N_{i_{4}}\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{1}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{3}}\rangle\langle\Omega_{i_{2}},\Omega_{i_{4}}\rangle

The details are very similar to (
215
)–(
217
), so we omit them and simply state the final bound:

\displaystyle C_{724}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}

Combining the bounds for
C_{721},C_{722},C_{723}
, and
C_{724}
yields

\displaystyle C_{7}\lesssim K\|\mu\|_{4}^{4}.

Combining the bounds for
C_{1}
–
C_{7}
proves the result.

∎

E.5
Proof of Lemma
17

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\ell,s}^{4}=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big(\sum_{i\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i,\ell}\sum_{r=1}^{N_{i}}\sum_{j}Z_{ijr}Z_{\ell js}\big)^{4}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}\\
j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}\big[Z_{i_{1}j_{1}r_{1}}Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{i_{2}j_{2}r_{2}}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{i_{3}j_{3}r_{3}}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{i_{4}j_{4}r_{4}}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in[\ell-1]}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}\\
j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}\big[Z_{i_{1}j_{1}r_{1}}Z_{i_{2}j_{2}r_{2}}Z_{i_{3}j_{3}r_{3}}Z_{i_{4}j_{4}r_{4}}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}\in[\ell-1]\\
r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{i_{1}j_{1}r_{1}}Z_{i_{2}j_{2}r_{2}}Z_{i_{3}j_{3}r_{3}}Z_{i_{4}j_{4}r_{4}}]

\displaystyle=:\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}]A_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}

(218)

In the summations above,
r_{t}
ranges over
[N_{i_{t}}]
.

Observe that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}]|\lesssim\begin{cases}\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{3}=j_{4}\\
\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{4}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{3},j_{4}\neq j_{1}\\
\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{3}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j_{1}=j_{2},j_{3}=j_{4},j_{1}\neq j_{3}\\
\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{3}}\Omega_{\ell j_{4}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j_{1}=j_{2},j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}\,\,dist.\\
\Omega_{\ell j_{1}}\Omega_{\ell j_{2}}\Omega_{\ell j_{3}}\Omega_{\ell j_{4}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\,\,dist.\\
\end{cases}

(219)

Up to permutation of the indices
j_{1},\ldots,j_{4}
, this accounts for all possible cases.

To proceed we also bound
A_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}
by casework on the number of distinct
j
indices. For brevity we define
\omega_{t}=(i_{t},r_{t})
and slightly abuse notation, letting
Z_{\omega_{t},j}=Z_{i_{t}jr_{t}}
. Further let
\mathcal{I}_{\ell}=\{\omega=(i,r):i\in[\ell],1\leq r\leq N_{i}\}
. Our goal is to control

\displaystyle A_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}=\sum_{\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},\omega_{4}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{2}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{4}}].

(220)

To do this, we study (
220
) in five cases that cover all possibilities (up to permutation of the indices
j_{1},\ldots,j_{4}
).

Case 1:

j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{3}=j_{4}
. Define
j=j_{1}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j}Z_{\omega_{2}j}Z_{\omega_{3}j}Z_{\omega_{4}j}]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j}^{4}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}=\omega_{3}=\omega_{4}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{3}j}^{2}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{3}j}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2},\omega_{3}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\end{cases}

(221)

Up to permutation of the indices
\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{4}
, this accounts for all cases such that (
221
) is nonvanishing. To be precise, by symmetry, it also holds that for all permutations
\pi:[4]\to[4]
that
if
\omega_{\pi(1)}=\omega_{\pi(2)},\omega_{\pi(3)}=\omega_{\pi(4)},\omega_{\pi(1)}\neq\omega_{\pi(3)}
, then

\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j}Z_{\omega_{2}j}Z_{\omega_{3}j}Z_{\omega_{4}j}]\lesssim\sigma_{i_{\pi(1)}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{\pi(3)}\ell}^{2}\Omega_{i_{\pi(1)}j}\Omega_{i_{\pi(3)}j}.

In all other cases besides those considered above, we have

\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j}Z_{\omega_{2}j}Z_{\omega_{3}j}Z_{\omega_{4}j}]=0

by independence.

Therefore,

\displaystyle A_{jjjj}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\omega\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i\ell}^{4}\Omega_{ij}+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\Omega_{i_{1}j}\Omega_{i_{3}j}

(222)

In the remaining Cases 2–6, we follow the same strategy of writing out bounds for

\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{2}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{4}}]

that cover all nonzero cases, up to permutation of the indices
\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{4}
.

Case 2:

j_{1}=j_{2}=j_{3},j_{1}\neq j_{4}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{4}}]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt=\begin{cases}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{3}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{4}}]\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}=\omega_{3}=\omega_{4}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2},\omega_{3}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\\
\end{cases}

(223)

Up to permutation of the indices
\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{4}
, this accounts for all cases such that (
223
) is nonvanishing. Thus

\displaystyle A_{j_{1},j_{1},j_{1},j_{4}}\lesssim\sum_{\omega\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i\ell}^{4}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}

(224)

Case 3:

j_{1}=j_{2},j_{3}=j_{4},j_{1}\neq j_{3}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{3}}]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{2}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{3}}^{2}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}=\omega_{3}=\omega_{4}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}^{2}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2},\omega_{3}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{3}}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{3}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{3}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{3},\omega_{2}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{2}.\end{cases}

(225)

Up to permutation of the indices
\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{4}
, this accounts for all cases such that (
225
) is nonvanishing. Thus by symmetry,

\displaystyle A_{j_{1},j_{1},j_{3},j_{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\omega\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}

(226)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}

Case 4:

j_{1}=j_{2}
and
j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}
distinct. We have

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{4}}]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{2}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}=\omega_{3}=\omega_{4}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2},\omega_{3}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{3}}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{2}j_{4}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{3},\omega_{2}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{2}\end{cases}

(227)

Up to permutation of the indices
\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{4}
, this accounts for all cases such that (
227
) is nonvanishing. Thus

\displaystyle A_{j_{1},j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\omega\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}

(228)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}.

Case 5:

j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}
distinct. For this final case, it holds that

\displaystyle\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}\sigma_{i_{2}\ell}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}\sigma_{i_{4}\ell}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{2}j_{2}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{4}j_{4}}]

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{2}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}=\omega_{3}=\omega_{4}\\
\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{1}}Z_{\omega_{1}j_{2}}\mathbb{E}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{3}}Z_{\omega_{3}j_{4}}\lesssim\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}&\text{ if }\omega_{1}=\omega_{2},\omega_{3}=\omega_{4},\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\\
\end{cases}

The above accounts for all nonzero cases, up to permutation of
\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3},\omega_{4}
. Hence

\displaystyle A_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}\lesssim\sum_{\omega\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{4}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{4}}+\sum_{\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{3}\in\mathcal{I}_{\ell-1}}\sigma_{i_{1}\ell}^{2}\sigma_{i_{3}\ell}^{2}\,\Omega_{i_{1}j_{1}}\Omega_{i_{1}j_{2}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{3}}\Omega_{i_{3}j_{4}}.

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{3}}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}]A_{j_{1},j_{1},j_{3},j_{3}}+\sum_{j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}\,dist.}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}]A_{j_{1},j_{1},j_{3},j_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\,dist.}\mathbb{E}[Z_{\ell j_{1}s}Z_{\ell j_{2}s}Z_{\ell j_{3}s}Z_{\ell j_{4}s}]A_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}}

\displaystyle=:F_{1\ell s}+F_{2\ell s}+F_{3\ell s}+F_{4\ell s}+F_{5\ell s}

-\Omega_{\ell j}\Omega_{\ell j^{\prime}}&\hskip 8.50012pt\text{ else}.\end{cases}

Recalling that
